# Graph Report - building-management  (2026-10-18)

## Corpus Check
- 2668 files · ~1,580,034 words
- Verdict: corpus is large enough that graph structure adds value.

## Summary
- 22793 nodes · 41159 edges · 1702 communities (1343 shown, 359 thin omitted)
- Extraction: 98% EXTRACTED · 2% INFERRED · 0% AMBIGUOUS · INFERRED: 1000 edges (avg confidence: 0.91)
- Token cost: 0 input · 0 output

## Graph Freshness
- Built from commit: `1d6f7104`
- Run `git rev-parse HEAD` and compare to check if the graph is stale.
- Run `graphify update .` after code changes (no API cost).
